([robertsolves/agent-commands](https://github.com/robertsolves/agent-commands/blob/HEAD/commands/find-bad-comments.md))
- **find-bandaids**: Find band-aid fixes left behind by AI sessions — swallowed errors, suppressions, magic delays, special-case patches — trace each to the root cause it papers over, ask the user which to fix properly, then implement the real fix. ([robertsolves/agent-commands](https://github.com/robertsolves/agent-commands/blob/HEAD/commands/find-bandaids.md))
